\displaystyle\sinh\left(\frac{3c_{2}}{2}+3\sqrt{2}y\right)

\displaystyle=\sinh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}y\right)\cosh(c_{2}+2\sqrt{2}y)+\cosh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}y\right)\sinh(c_{2}+2\sqrt{2}y)\,,

\displaystyle\geq\sinh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}y\right)+\sinh(c_{2}+2\sqrt{2}y)\,,

\sinh\left(c_{2}+2\sqrt{2}y\right)=2\sinh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}y\right)\cosh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}y\right)\geq 2\sinh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}y\right)

and
c_{2}<0
together with the fact that
\sinh
is increasing.

We have just proved the following statement: if
\mathpzc{d}(x_{0},x)\leq r
, then

\forall\,y\in[0,1],\quad\gamma(y)\in\{x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}\>:\>\mathpzc{d}(x_{0},x^{\prime})\leq r\}\,.

This concludes the proof of Lemma
5.2
.

H.3.2
Proof of Lemma
5.3

Let
r,\Delta>0
. Assume that
\mathcal{X}\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},u_{\max}]^{d}
. The semi-distance
\mathpzc{d}
is defined by (
11
).

Control of the variance of the geodesics:
Let
x_{j}^{0}\in\mathcal{X}
. For
x\in B_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{j}^{0},r)\cap\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},u_{\max}]^{d}
, we have
|t_{k}-t_{j,k}^{0}|\leq r\sqrt{2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2}}
for all
k\in\{1,\ldots,d\}
.
We deduce that

B_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{j}^{0},r)\cap(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},u_{\max}]^{d})\subset\left(\bigtimes_{k=1}^{d}[t_{j,k}^{0}\pm r\sqrt{2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2}}]\right)\times[u_{\min},u_{\max}]^{d}

where
\bigtimes_{k=1}^{d}
denotes the
d
-ary Cartesian product.

For any
k\in\{1,\ldots,d\}
, the semicircle
\gamma_{k}=(\gamma_{t_{k}},\gamma_{u_{k}})
(geodesic in dimension 1) connecting the points
(t_{j,k}^{0}-r\sqrt{2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2}},u_{\max})
and
(t_{j,k}^{0},u_{\max})
satisfies
\gamma_{u_{k}}(y)^{2}\leq u_{\max}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}r^{2}(2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2})
. This follows because the square of the radius of the semicircle is
u_{\max}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}r^{2}(2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2})
(see Lemma
H.2
). The same holds for the semicircle connecting
(t_{j,k}^{0}+r\sqrt{2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2}},u_{\max})
and
(t_{j,k}^{0},u_{\max})
.

Any geodesic connecting
(t_{j,k}^{0},u_{j,k}^{0})
with a point of
[t_{j,k}^{0}-r\sqrt{2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2}}\,,\,t_{j,k}^{0}+r\sqrt{2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2}}]\times[u_{\min},u_{\max}]
is below these geodesics.

To see why it is the case, one can note that 2 geodesics in our model have at most 1 intersection point, or their union is a geodesic. So a geodesic between points of
[t_{j,k}^{0}-r\sqrt{2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2}}\,,\,t_{j,k}^{0}]\times[u_{\min},u_{\max}]
cannot intersect twice the semicircle connecting
(t_{j,k}^{0}-r\sqrt{2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2}},u_{\max})
and
(t_{j,k}^{0},u_{\max})
. An analogous statement holds when considering
[t_{j,k}^{0}\,,\,t_{j,k}^{0}+r\sqrt{2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2}}]\times[u_{\min},u_{\max}]
.

Using Lemma
H.3
, we deduce that

\mathcal{G}_{x_{j}^{0}}(B_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{j}^{0},r)\cap(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},u_{\max}]^{d}))\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\left[u_{\min},\sqrt{u_{\max}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}r^{2}(2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2})}\right]^{d}\,.

Pseudo-quasi triangle inequality:
Let
x,x^{\prime},\tilde{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},\tilde{u}_{\max}]^{d}
, where
\tilde{u}_{\max}
is not necessarily equal to
u_{\max}
.
We have

\frac{u_{k}^{2}+u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}{\sqrt{2u_{k}^{2}+\tau^{2}}\sqrt{2u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}\leq\frac{2\tilde{u}_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2}}{2u_{\min}^{2}+\tau^{2}}\leq\frac{\tilde{u}_{\max}^{2}}{u_{\min}^{2}}

using that
\tau\in\mathbb{R}^{+}\mapsto\frac{2\tilde{u}_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2}}{2u_{\min}^{2}+\tau^{2}}
is decreasing (because
u_{\min}\leq\tilde{u}_{\max}
).
Moreover, using the triangle inequality for the
\ell^{2}
-norm in dimension
d
and that
u_{k}^{2}+\tilde{u}_{k}^{2}+\tau^{2}
and
u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tilde{u}_{k}^{2}+\tau^{2}
are smaller than
\frac{\tilde{u}_{\max}^{2}}{u_{\min}^{2}}(u_{k}^{2}+u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})
, we get

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{d}\frac{(t_{k}-t_{k}^{\prime})^{2}}{u_{k}^{2}+u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{d}\frac{(t_{k}-\tilde{t}_{k})^{2}}{u_{k}^{2}+u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}+\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{d}\frac{(t_{k}^{\prime}-\tilde{t}_{k})^{2}}{u_{k}^{2}+u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}\,,

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{u}_{\max}}{u_{\min}}\left(\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{d}\frac{(t_{k}-\tilde{t}_{k})^{2}}{u_{k}^{2}+\tilde{u}_{k}^{2}+\tau^{2}}}+\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{d}\frac{(t_{k}^{\prime}-\tilde{t}_{k})^{2}}{u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tilde{u}_{k}^{2}+\tau^{2}}}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{u}_{\max}}{u_{\min}}\left(\mathpzc{d}(x,\tilde{x})+\mathpzc{d}(x^{\prime},\tilde{x})\right)\,.

Hence

\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\leq\frac{\tilde{u}_{\max}}{u_{\min}}\left(\mathpzc{d}(x,\tilde{x})+\mathpzc{d}(x^{\prime},\tilde{x})\right)+\sqrt{d\ln\left(\frac{\tilde{u}_{\max}^{2}}{u_{\min}^{2}}\right)}\,.

So
\mathpzc{d}(x,\tilde{x})\geq\frac{u_{\min}}{\tilde{u}_{\max}}\left(\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})-\sqrt{d\ln\left(\frac{\tilde{u}_{\max}^{2}}{u_{\min}^{2}}\right)}\right)-\mathpzc{d}(x^{\prime},\tilde{x})
.

Conclusion:

Let
x_{j}^{0},x_{i}^{0}\in\mathcal{X}
. Firstly, replacing
\tilde{u}_{\max}
by
u_{\max}
in the pseudo-quasi triangle inequality, for
\tilde{x}\in B_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{j}^{0},\Delta)\cap\mathcal{X}
, if
\frac{u_{\min}}{u_{\max}}\left(\mathpzc{d}(x_{i}^{0},x_{j}^{0})-\sqrt{d\ln\left(\frac{u_{\max}^{2}}{u_{\min}^{2}}\right)}\right)-\mathpzc{d}(\tilde{x},x_{j}^{0})>\Delta
we have
\mathpzc{d}(x_{i}^{0},\tilde{x})>\Delta
. Note also that
\mathpzc{d}(\tilde{x},x_{j}^{0})<\Delta
, hence it suffices that
\mathpzc{d}(x_{j}^{0},x_{i}^{0})\geq 2\frac{u_{\max}}{u_{\min}}\Delta+\sqrt{d\ln\left(\frac{u_{\max}^{2}}{u_{\min}^{2}}\right)}
for the open balls
\mathring{B}_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{j}^{0},\Delta)\cap\mathcal{X}
to be disjoint.

Secondly, for
\tilde{x}\in\mathcal{G}_{x_{j}^{0}}(B_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{j}^{0},r)\cap\mathcal{X})
, taking
\tilde{u}_{\max}=\sqrt{u_{\max}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}r^{2}(2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2})}
we have
\mathpzc{d}(x_{i}^{0},\tilde{x})\geq\Delta
as soon as
\frac{u_{\min}}{\tilde{u}_{\max}}\left(\mathpzc{d}(x_{i}^{0},x_{j}^{0})-\sqrt{d\ln\left(\frac{\tilde{u}_{\max}^{2}}{u_{\min}^{2}}\right)}\right)\geq\Delta+r
.
We used that
\mathcal{G}_{x_{j}^{0}}(B_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{j}^{0},r))\subset B_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{j}^{0},r)
, so
\mathpzc{d}(\tilde{x},x_{j}^{0})\leq r
(see Lemma
5.2
).
This condition can be rewritten as
\mathpzc{d}(x_{i}^{0},x_{j}^{0})\geq\frac{\tilde{u}_{\max}}{u_{\min}}(\Delta+r)+\sqrt{d\ln\left(\frac{u_{\max}^{2}}{u_{\min}^{2}}\right)}
.
We proved that if

\min_{i\neq j}\mathpzc{d}(x_{i}^{0},x_{j}^{0})\geq\max\left\{\frac{\sqrt{u_{\max}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}r^{2}(2u_{\max}^{2}+\tau^{2})}}{u_{\min}}(\Delta+r)\>,\>2\frac{u_{\max}}{u_{\min}}\Delta\right\}+\sqrt{d\ln\left(\frac{u_{\max}^{2}}{u_{\min}^{2}}\right)}\eqcolon\Delta_{\tau}\,,

then the open balls
\mathring{B}_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{j}^{0},\Delta)\cap\mathcal{X}
are disjoint, and for all
j\neq i
, the ball
\mathcal{G}_{x_{j}^{0}}(B_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{j}^{0},r)\cap\mathcal{X})
does not intersect
\mathring{B}_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{i}^{0},\Delta)
.

H.3.3
Local control with the semi-distance

Lemma H.4

(Local control of
\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}
with the semi-distance, lower bound,
d=1
)
.

Let
x_{0},x\in\mathbb{R}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)
. If
\mathpzc{d}(x_{0},x)\leq r
, then

\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x_{0},x)^{2}\geq\frac{\mathpzc{d}(x_{0},x)^{2}}{\tilde{\varepsilon}_{3}}

for
\tilde{\varepsilon}_{3}\geq 1+\frac{1}{R(r)}
with
R(r)
defined by (
57
) below.

Proof.

Let
x_{0}=(t_{0},u_{0}),x=(t,u)\in\mathbb{R}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)
such that
r_{e}\coloneq\mathpzc{d}(x_{0},x)\leq r
. Without loss of generality, we assume that
u_{0}\leq u
.

We examine the case where
u
is “far” from
u_{0}
, and then the case where
t
is far from
t_{0}
and
u,u_{0}
are close.

Bounds on
B_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{0},r)
, exhibition of 2 cases:
Since
\mathpzc{d}(x_{0},x)^{2}=\frac{(t_{0}-t)^{2}}{u^{2}+u_{0}^{2}+\tau^{2}}+\ln\left(\frac{u^{2}+u_{0}^{2}+\tau^{2}}{\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\sqrt{2u_{0}^{2}+\tau}}\right)=r_{e}^{2}
, we have that for all
0<w<1
,

\frac{(t_{0}-t)^{2}}{u^{2}+u_{0}^{2}+\tau^{2}}\geq wr_{e}^{2}

(50)

(first case) or

\frac{u^{2}+u_{0}^{2}+\tau^{2}}{\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\sqrt{2u_{0}^{2}+\tau^{2}}}\geq e^{(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}

(second case). The second case can be rewritten as

\sqrt{u^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}}\geq\sqrt{u_{0}^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}}(e^{(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}-1})

(51)

(see (
34
)).

Assuming the first case holds and the second case does not:
Provided that (
50
) holds, then
t_{0}\neq t
and the geodesic connecting
x
and
x_{0}
is a semicircle (see Lemma
H.2
).
We suppose additionally that we are not in the second case, namely that

\sqrt{u^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}}\leq\sqrt{u_{0}^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}}(e^{(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}-1})\,.

In particular, using (
35
) we have

\frac{|u_{0}^{2}-u^{2}|}{u_{0}^{2}+u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\leq\sqrt{e^{2(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}-1}\,.

(52)

Denoting
l
the arc length (
i.e
.

l=\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})
), using (
48
) and (
49
) we have

\frac{|\tanh(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}l)-\tanh(\frac{c_{2}}{2})|}{\sqrt{2}}=|c_{1}||t-t_{0}|

(53)

for some
c_{2}
and
c_{1}
verifying

\frac{1}{2c_{1}^{2}}-\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}=\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{-(t-t_{0})^{2}+u_{0}^{2}-u^{2}}{t-t_{0}}\right)^{2}+u_{0}^{2}\,.

According to (
50
) and (
52
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|c_{1}||t-t_{0}|}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}\left(-1+\frac{u_{0}^{2}-u^{2}}{(t-t_{0})^{2}}\right)^{2}+\frac{2u_{0}^{2}+\tau^{2}}{(t-t_{0})^{2}}}\,,

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{(u_{0}^{2}-u^{2})^{2}}{(t-t_{0})^{4}}+\frac{u_{0}^{2}+u^{2}+\tau^{2}}{(t-t_{0})^{2}}}\,,

(54)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{e^{2(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}-1}{2w^{2}r_{e}^{4}}+\frac{1}{wr_{e}^{2}}}\,,

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{w}r_{e}}\sqrt{\frac{r^{2}}{2}+\frac{1-w}{w}\frac{e^{2r^{2}}-1}{2r^{2}}+1}

where we used that
y\mapsto e^{2y}-1
is convex along with
(1-w)r_{e}^{2}\leq r^{2}
, and
wr_{e}^{2}\leq r^{2}
.

So as
\tanh
is 1-Lipschitz,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})=l

\displaystyle\geq|c_{1}||t-t_{0}|\,,

\displaystyle\geq\frac{r_{e}\sqrt{w}}{\sqrt{1+\frac{r^{2}}{2}+\frac{1-w}{w}\frac{e^{2r^{2}}-1}{2r^{2}}}}\,.

(55)

Assuming the second case holds:
If (
51
) holds, we must consider the 2 possible geodesics (
c.f
.
Lemma
H.2
).

If
t=t_{0}
, the geodesic is of the form
\left(c_{3},\ \sqrt{-\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}+\frac{c_{1}^{2}}{2}e^{\sqrt{8}y}}\right)
. As
\tilde{\gamma}(0)=x_{0}
we have
c_{1}^{2}=2u_{0}^{2}+\tau^{2}
. Since
\tilde{\gamma}_{u}(l)=u
, we have
e^{\sqrt{8}l}=\frac{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}{2u_{0}^{2}+\tau^{2}}
from which we deduce that

\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x_{0},x)=l=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\ln\left(\frac{\sqrt{u^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}}}{\sqrt{u_{0}^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}}}\right)\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\ln\left(e^{(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}-1}\right)\,.

If
t\neq t_{0}
,
\tilde{\gamma}_{u}
is of the form
\sqrt{-\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}+\frac{1}{2\cosh^{2}{\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}y\right)}c_{1}^{2}}}
. Using that
\tilde{\gamma}_{u}(0)=u_{0}
and
\tilde{\gamma}_{u}(l)=u
, we get
\frac{\cosh^{2}{\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}\right)}}{\cosh^{2}{\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}l\right)}}=\frac{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}{2u_{0}^{2}+\tau^{2}}
, from which we deduce using (
51
) that

\frac{\cosh{\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}\right)}}{\cosh{\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}l\right)}}\geq e^{(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}-1}\,.

As

\frac{\cosh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}\right)}{\cosh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}l\right)}=\cosh(\sqrt{2}l)-\frac{\sinh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}\right)\sinh(\sqrt{2}l)}{\cosh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}\right)}\leq\cosh(\sqrt{2}l)+|\sinh(\sqrt{2}l)|=e^{|\sqrt{2}l|}\,,

it comes that
e^{\sqrt{2}l}\geq e^{(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}-1}
leading again to

l\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\ln\left(e^{(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2(1-w)r_{e}^{2}}-1}\right)\,.

As
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\ln\left(e^{y^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2y^{2}}-1}\right)\geq y
for all
y\in\mathbb{R}
, we get for this second case

\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})\geq\sqrt{1-w}r_{e}\,.

(56)

Conclusion:
We now choose
0<w<1
depending only on
r
to balance the bounds of the two cases (namely, (
55
) and (
56
)). Writing
X=\frac{1-w}{w}
, we want to pick
w
such that

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-w}=\frac{\sqrt{w}}{\sqrt{1+\frac{r^{2}}{2}+\frac{1-w}{w}\frac{e^{2r^{2}}-1}{2r^{2}}}}

\displaystyle\iff

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2r^{2}}-1}{2r^{2}}X^{2}+\left(1+\frac{r^{2}}{2}\right)X-1=0\,.

This polynomial has exactly 1 positive root

R(r)=\frac{-\left(1+\frac{r^{2}}{2}\right)+\sqrt{\left(1+\frac{r^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}+2\frac{e^{2r^{2}}-1}{r^{2}}}}{\frac{e^{2r^{2}}-1}{r^{2}}}\,.

(57)

We can take
w_{r}=\frac{1}{R(r)+1}
. Then

\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x_{0},x)\geq\sqrt{1-w_{r}}r_{e}=\sqrt{\frac{R(r)}{1+R(r)}}r_{e}\,.

∎

Lemma H.5

(Local control of
\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}
with the semi-distance, lower bound,
d\geq 1
)
.

Let
x_{0},x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
. If
\mathpzc{d}(x_{0},x)\leq r
, then

\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x_{0},x)^{2}\geq\frac{\mathpzc{d}(x_{0},x)^{2}}{\tilde{\varepsilon}_{3}}

for
\tilde{\varepsilon}_{3}\geq 1+\frac{1}{R(r)}
with
R(r)
defined by (
57
).

Proof.

Let
x_{0},x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
such that
\mathpzc{d}(x_{0},x)\leq r
. For all
k\in\{1,\ldots,d\}
,
\mathpzc{d}(x_{0,k},x_{k})\leq r
. Lemma
H.4
gives
\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x_{k},x_{0,k})^{2}\geq\frac{\mathpzc{d}(x_{0,k},x_{k})^{2}}{1+\frac{1}{R(r)}}
. We conclude using that

\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})^{2}=\sum_{k=1}^{d}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x_{k},x_{0,k})^{2}\geq\sum_{k=1}^{d}\frac{\mathpzc{d}(x_{0,k},x_{k})^{2}}{1+\frac{1}{R(r)}}=\frac{\mathpzc{d}(x_{0},x)^{2}}{1+\frac{1}{R(r)}}\,.

∎

Lemma H.6

(Local control of
\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}
with the semi-distance, upper bound,
d=1
)
.

Let
x,x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)
. If
\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{0})<\sqrt{2}
, then
\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})^{2}\leq F(\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{0}))
(
F
defined by (
62
) below).

Proof.

Let
x,x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)
. Without loss of generality, we can assume that
u_{0}\leq u
.
We denote
l=\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})
and
r_{e}=\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{0})
. The proof is close to that of Lemma
H.4
.

First case:
If
t\neq t_{0}
, the geodesic connecting
x
and
x_{0}
is a semicircle. Recalling (
53
) and (
54
), we have

\frac{|\tanh(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}l)-\tanh(\frac{c_{2}}{2})|}{\sqrt{2}|c_{1}|}=|t-t_{0}|

for some
c_{2}
and
c_{1}
verifying

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|c_{1}||t-t_{0}|}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{(u_{0}^{2}-u^{2})^{2}}{(t-t_{0})^{4}}+\frac{u_{0}^{2}+u^{2}+\tau^{2}}{(t-t_{0})^{2}}}\,,

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{r_{e}}\,.

Hence

\left|\tanh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}l\right)-\tanh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}\right)\right|\leq\sqrt{2}r_{e}\,.

(58)

Furthermore, using
\frac{u_{0}^{2}+u^{2}+\tau^{2}}{\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\sqrt{2u_{0}^{2}+\tau^{2}}}\leq e^{r_{e}^{2}}
together with (
34
) and recalling that
u_{0}\leq u
, we have

1\leq\frac{\cosh{\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}\right)}}{\cosh{\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}l\right)}}=\sqrt{\frac{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}{2u_{0}^{2}+\tau^{2}}}\leq e^{r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r_{e}^{2}}-1}\,.

(59)

Using (
59
) along with the equality
\frac{\cosh(A+B)}{\cosh(A)}=\cosh(B)+\tanh(A)\sinh(B)
, we deduce that

\cosh\left(\sqrt{2}l\right)+\tanh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}\right)\sinh\left(\sqrt{2}l\right)\leq 1

and that

\cosh\left(\sqrt{2}l\right)-\tanh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}l\right)\sinh\left(\sqrt{2}l\right)\leq e^{r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r_{e}^{2}}-1}\,.

It comes

2\cosh\left(\sqrt{2}l\right)+\left(\tanh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}\right)-\tanh\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}+\sqrt{2}l\right)\right)\sinh\left(\sqrt{2}l\right)\leq e^{r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r_{e}^{2}}-1}+1\,,

so

2\cosh\left(\sqrt{2}l\right)-\sinh\left(\sqrt{2}l\right)\sqrt{2}r_{e}\leq e^{r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r_{e}^{2}}-1}+1

where we have used (
58
).
As
\cosh\geq 0
and
\frac{\sinh(\sqrt{2}l)}{\cosh(\sqrt{2}l)}\leq 1
, we get

\cosh(\sqrt{2}l)\left(1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}r_{e}\right)\leq\frac{1}{2}\left(e^{r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r_{e}^{2}}-1}+1\right)\,.

For
r_{e}<\sqrt{2}
we get

l^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{arcosh}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}\left(e^{r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r_{e}^{2}}-1}+1\right)}{1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}r_{e}}\right)^{2}\,.

(60)

Second case:
If
t_{0}=t
, using (
47
) we get
e^{\sqrt{8}l}=\frac{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}{2u_{0}^{2}+\tau^{2}}\leq(e^{r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r_{e}^{2}}-1})^{2}
so

l^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\ln(e^{r_{e}^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r_{e}^{2}}-1})^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{arcosh}(e^{r_{e}^{2}})^{2}\,.

(61)

Comparison of the bounds found, conclusion:

Let
0\leq r_{e}<\sqrt{2}
. As
1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}r_{e}\leq 1
and
\sqrt{e^{2r_{e}^{2}}-1}\geq e^{r_{e}^{2}}-1
, the bound found for the first case (see (
60
)) is larger than the one found for the second case (see (
61
)).

So if
\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{0})<\sqrt{2}
,
\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})^{2}\leq F(\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{0}))
with

F:y\in\mathbb{R}^{+}\mapsto\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{arcosh}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}\left(e^{y^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2y^{2}}-1}+1\right)}{1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}y}\right)^{2}\,.

(62)

∎

Lemma H.7

(Local control of
\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}
with the semi-distance, upper bound,
d\geq 1
)
.

Let
x,x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
. If
\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{0})<\sqrt{2}
, then
\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})^{2}\leq dF(\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{0}))
(
F
defined by (
62
)).

Proof.

Let
x,x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
such that
\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{0})=r_{e}<\sqrt{2}
. As for all
k=1,\ldots,d
,
\mathpzc{d}(x_{k},x_{0,k})\leq\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{0})
,
by Lemma
H.6
it comes that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{d}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x_{k},x_{0,k})^{2}\,

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{d}F(\mathpzc{d}(x_{k},x_{0,k}))\,,

\displaystyle\leq dF(\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{0}))

where we used that
F
is non-decreasing.
∎

H.4
Proof of Lemma
5.4

We use Lemma
H.5
. It comes that we can choose
\tilde{\varepsilon}_{3}=1+\frac{1}{R\left(\frac{0.3025}{\sqrt{d}}\right)}
with
R
defined by (
57
) in the appendix. We aim to find a more interpretable parameter.
Remark that
r\in\mathbb{R}^{+}\mapsto\frac{e^{2r^{2}}-1}{r^{2}}
is increasing, along with
\frac{e^{2r^{2}}-1}{r^{2}}\geq 2
. We deduce that for all
0<r\leq 0.3025
,

R(r)\geq\frac{-\left(1+\frac{0.3025^{2}}{2}\right)+\sqrt{5}}{\frac{e^{2\times 0.3025^{2}}-1}{0.3025^{2}}}
.
So
1+\frac{1}{R\left(\frac{0.3025}{\sqrt{d}}\right)}\leq 2.84
(see
(
Giard 2025
, Section III)
).

Appendix I
Proof of Theorem
5.2

Construction of the certificates by solving a linear system

We give explicit formulas for
\eta
,
\eta_{j}
of Theorem
5.2
. These certificates are of the form (
18
). In the following, we write
\eta=\eta_{\alpha,\beta}
and
\eta_{j}=\eta_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}
.
Recalling Definitions
3.2
and
3.3
, we want that for all
j=1,\ldots,s
,
\eta_{\alpha,\beta}(x_{j}^{0})=1
and
\nabla\eta_{\alpha,\beta}(x_{j}^{0})=0_{2d}
. We also want
\eta_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}(x_{j}^{0})=1
,
\eta_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}(x_{l}^{0})=0
for all
l\neq j
, and
\nabla\eta_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}(x_{l}^{0})=0_{2d}
.

These constraints translate to linear systems. Writing
e_{j}
the vector of size
s
containing a
1
at position
j
, and zeros elsewhere, along with
1_{s}=\sum_{j=1}^{s}e_{j}
, we want to solve

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Upsilon\begin{pmatrix}\alpha\\
\beta\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}1_{s}\\
0_{s2d}\end{pmatrix}\eqcolon\bm{u}_{s}\quad\text{and}\quad\Upsilon\begin{pmatrix}\alpha^{j}\\
\beta^{j}\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}e_{j}\\
0_{s2d}\end{pmatrix}\eqcolon\bm{u}_{s}^{j}\quad\forall\,j=1,\ldots,s\\
&\text{where}\quad\Upsilon=\begin{pmatrix}(K_{\rm norm}(x_{i}^{0},x_{j}^{0}))_{i,j=1,\ldots,s}&(\nabla_{1}K_{\rm norm}(x_{i}^{0},x_{j}^{0}))_{i,j=1,\ldots,s}^{T}\\
(\nabla_{2}K_{\rm norm}(x_{i}^{0},x_{j}^{0}))_{i,j=1,\ldots,s}&(\nabla_{1}\nabla_{2}K_{\rm norm}(x_{i}^{0},x_{j}^{0}))_{i,j=1,\ldots,s}\end{pmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{s(1+2d)\times s(1+2d)}\,.\end{split}

(63)

The following lemma entails that these linear systems admit a solution provided that a minimal separation condition holds.

Lemma I.1
.

Let
\{x_{j}^{0}\}_{j=1}^{s}\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
. Assume that
K_{\rm norm}
verifies Definition
5.2
. If
\min_{i\neq j}\mathpzc{d}(x_{i}^{0},x_{j}^{0})\penalty\ \geq\penalty\ \Delta
, then
\Upsilon
(see (
63
)) is invertible.

Moreover,
\begin{pmatrix}\alpha\\
\beta\end{pmatrix}=\Upsilon^{-1}\bm{u}_{s}
and

\begin{pmatrix}\alpha^{j}\\
\beta^{j}\end{pmatrix}=\Upsilon^{-1}\bm{u}_{s}^{j}
are well-defined and verify, for all
j=1,\ldots,s
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left\lVert\alpha\right\rVert_{\infty}\vee\left\lVert\alpha^{j}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-2h}\,,\\
&\max_{l=1,\ldots,s}\left\lVert\beta_{l}\right\rVert_{x_{l}^{0}}\vee\max_{l=1,\ldots,s}\left\lVert\beta_{l}^{j}\right\rVert_{x_{l}^{0}}\leq 4h\\
\text{and}\quad&\left\lVert\alpha-1_{s}\right\rVert_{\infty}\vee|\alpha_{j}^{j}-1|\vee\max_{l\neq j}|\alpha_{l}^{j}|\leq\frac{2h}{1-2h}\,,\end{split}

(64)

where
h=\frac{1}{64}\min\left(\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}(r)}{B_{0}},\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}(r)}{B_{2}}\right)
.

The proof can be found in
(
Poon et al. 2023
, pp. 269-270)
. This proof explicitly requires to handle normalized kernels. This motivates the introduction of
W
in (

P
κ

).

Controlling the certificates via the kernel

We provide controls on the certificates we constructed in Lemma
I.1
on the far and near regions, in order to show that they are non-degenerate (Definitions
3.2
and
3.3
). To do so, we use Taylor expansions on Fisher-Rao geodesics along with bounds on the kernel stemming from the LPC (Definition
5.2
).

Lemma I.2
.

Let
r>0
and
x_{0}\in\mathcal{X}\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
. We define

\mathcal{X}_{0}^{near}(r)\coloneq\{x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}\>:\>\mathpzc{d}(x^{\prime},x_{0})\leq r\}\,.

Assume that there exists
\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}>0

such that, for all
x\in\mathcal{G}_{x_{0}}(\mathcal{X}_{0}^{near}(r))
and
v\in\mathbb{R}^{2d}
,

-K_{\rm norm}^{(02)}(x_{0},x)[v,v]\geq\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}\left\lVert v\right\rVert_{x}^{2}\quad\text{and}\quad\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(02)}(x_{0},x)\right\rVert_{x}\leq B_{02}\,.

Let
\eta:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
be a
\mathcal{C}^{2}
function. Then the following holds:

(i)

If
\eta(x_{0})=0
,
\nabla\eta(x_{0})=0
and
\left\lVert D_{2}[\eta](x)\right\rVert_{x}\leq\delta
for all
x\in\mathcal{G}_{x_{0}}(\mathcal{X}_{0}^{near}(r))
, then
|\eta(x)|\leq\delta\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})^{2}
for all
x\in\mathcal{X}_{0}^{near}(r)
.

(ii)

Let
a\in\{-1,1\}
. If
\eta(x_{0})=a
,
\nabla\eta(x_{0})=0
and
\left\lVert aD_{2}[\eta](x)-K_{\rm norm}^{(02)}(x_{0},x)\right\rVert_{x}\leq\delta
for all
x\in\mathcal{G}_{x_{0}}(\mathcal{X}_{0}^{near}(r))
, for some
\delta<\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}
, then

1-\frac{B_{02}+\delta}{2}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})^{2}\leq a\eta(x)\leq 1-\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}-\delta}{2}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})^{2}\quad\forall\,x\in\mathcal{X}_{0}^{near}(r)\,.

Proof.

This proof is based on
(
Poon et al. 2023
, Lemma 2)
. We only show
(ii)
, as the proof for
(i)
is similar.

Let
x\in\mathcal{X}_{0}^{near}(r)
. We denote by
\gamma
the geodesic for the metric
\mathfrak{g}
between
x_{0}
and
x
, parametrized between
0
and
1
. We refer to Section
H.2
for a description of the geodesic properties and related notations. By definition,
\gamma(y)\in\mathcal{G}_{x_{0}}(\mathcal{X}_{0}^{near}(r))
for all
y\in[0,1]
. Hence, for all
y\in[0,1]
,

\left\lVert aD_{2}[\eta](\gamma(y))-K_{\rm norm}^{(02)}(x_{0},\gamma(y))\right\rVert_{\gamma(y)}\leq\delta\quad\text{and}\quad-K_{\rm norm}^{(02)}(x_{0},\gamma(y))[\dot{\gamma}(y),\dot{\gamma}(y)]\geq\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}\left\lVert\dot{\gamma}(y)\right\rVert_{\gamma(y)}^{2}\,,

from which we deduce that

aD_{2}[\eta](\gamma(y))[\dot{\gamma}(y),\dot{\gamma}(y)]\leq(-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}+\delta)\left\lVert\dot{\gamma}(y)\right\rVert_{\gamma(y)}^{2}\,.

By a Taylor expansion, we get

\displaystyle a\eta(x)

\displaystyle=a\eta(x_{0})+a\nabla\eta(x_{0})^{T}\dot{\gamma}(0)+\int_{0}^{1}(1-y)aD_{2}[\eta](\gamma(y))[\dot{\gamma}(y),\dot{\gamma}(y)]\,\text{d}y\,,

\displaystyle=1+\int_{0}^{1}(1-y)aD_{2}[\eta](\gamma(y))[\dot{\gamma}(y),\dot{\gamma}(y)]\,\text{d}y\,,

\displaystyle\leq 1-(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}-\delta)\int_{0}^{1}(1-y)\left\lVert\dot{\gamma}(y)\right\rVert_{\gamma(y)}^{2}\,\text{d}y\,,

\displaystyle=1-\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}-\delta}{2}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})^{2}\,.

Using the same reasoning, we also have
a\eta(x)\geq 1-\frac{B_{02}+\delta}{2}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})^{2}
.

Note that we do not have (unlike in
(
Poon et al. 2023
, Lemma 2)
) the bound
a\eta(x)\geq-1+\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}-\delta}{2}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{0})^{2}
. This is not a problem in our framework: as we work with nonnegative measures, we do not need to control the negative part of the certificate.
∎

Theorem I.1
.

Let
\{x_{j}^{0}\}_{j=1}^{s}\subset\mathcal{X}\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},u_{\max}]^{d}
. Assume that
K_{\rm norm}
verifies Definition
5.2
.

If
\min_{i\neq j}\mathpzc{d}(x_{i}^{0},x_{j}^{0})\geq\Delta_{\tau}
(defined in Lemma
5.3
), then the certificates constructed in Lemma
I.1
are non-degenerate. The global certificate
\eta_{\alpha,\beta}
is
(\frac{7}{8}\bar{\varepsilon}_{0},\frac{15}{32}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2},r)
-non-degenerate and the local certificate
\eta_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}
is
(\frac{7}{8}\bar{\varepsilon}_{0},\frac{B_{02}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}/16}{2},r)
-non-degenerate.

Proof.

This proof is largely based on
(
Poon et al. 2023
, pp. 270-241)
.
First remark that Lemmas
I.1
and
5.3
hold under these assumptions. We denote
h=\frac{1}{64}\min\left(\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}(r)}{B_{0}},\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}(r)}{B_{2}}\right)
.

Control on the far region:
Let
x\in\mathcal{X}^{far}(r)
. As the open balls
\mathring{B}_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{i}^{0},\Delta)\cap\mathcal{X}
are disjoint (Lemma
5.3
), there exists at most one index
j
such that
\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{j}^{0})<\Delta
and for all
i\neq j
,
\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{i}^{0})\geq\Delta
.
So using bounds displayed in Lemma
I.1
(see (
64
)),

\displaystyle|\eta_{\alpha,\beta}(x)|

\displaystyle=\left|\alpha_{j}K_{\rm norm}(x_{j}^{0},x)+\sum_{j\neq i}\alpha_{i}K_{\rm norm}(x_{i}^{0},x)+\beta_{j}^{T}K_{\rm norm}^{(10)}(x_{j}^{0},x)+\sum_{j\neq i}\beta_{i}^{T}K_{\rm norm}^{(10)}(x_{i}^{0},x)\right|\,,

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\alpha\right\rVert_{\infty}\left(|K_{\rm norm}(x_{j}^{0},x)|+\sum_{j\neq i}|K_{\rm norm}(x_{i}^{0},x)|\right)+\max_{i}\left\lVert\beta_{i}\right\rVert_{x_{i}^{0}}\left(\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(10)}(x_{j}^{0},x)\right\rVert_{x_{j}^{0}}+\sum_{j\neq i}\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(10)}(x_{i}^{0},x)\right\rVert_{x_{i}^{0}}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2h}(1-\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}+h)+4h(B_{10}+h)\,,

\displaystyle\leq 1-\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}-3h}{1-2h}+4h(B_{10}+h)\,,

\displaystyle\leq 1-\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}+3h+4h(B_{10}+h)\,,

\displaystyle\leq 1-\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}+3\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}}{64}+4\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}}{64}+4\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}}{64^{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\leq 1-\frac{7}{8}\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}\,.

We can apply the same reasoning to show that
|\eta_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}(x)|\leq 1-\frac{7}{8}\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}
.

Controls on the near regions:

Let
x\in\mathcal{G}_{x_{j}^{0}}(\mathcal{X}_{j}^{near}(r))
. We have

D_{2}[\eta_{\alpha,\beta}](x)=K^{(02)}_{\rm norm}(x_{j}^{0},x)+(\alpha_{j}-1)K^{(02)}_{\rm norm}(x_{j}^{0},x)+\sum_{i\neq j}\alpha_{i}K^{(02)}_{\rm norm}(x_{i}^{0},x)+[\beta_{j}]K^{(12)}_{\rm norm}(x_{j}^{0},x)+\sum_{i\neq j}[\beta_{i}]K^{(12)}_{\rm norm}(x_{i}^{0},x)\,.

As
\mathcal{G}_{x_{j}^{0}}(B_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{j}^{0},r)\cap\mathcal{X})
is disjoint from
\mathring{B}_{\mathpzc{d}}(x_{i}^{0},\Delta)
for
i\neq j
(Lemma
5.3
), we have
\mathpzc{d}(x,x_{i}^{0})\geq\Delta
for all
i\neq j
. Using Lemma
I.1
, it comes

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{2}[\eta_{\alpha,\beta}](x)-K^{(02)}_{\rm norm}(x_{j}^{0},x)\right\rVert_{x}

\displaystyle\leq|\alpha_{j}-1|B_{02}+\left\lVert\alpha\right\rVert_{\infty}h+\max_{i}\left\lVert\beta_{i}\right\rVert_{x_{i}^{0}}(B_{12}+h)\,,

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2h}{1-2h}B_{02}+\frac{h}{1-2h}+4h(B_{12}+h)\,,

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}}{16}\,.

Using Lemma
I.2
with
\delta=\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}}{16}
, we deduce that for all
x\in\mathcal{X}_{j}^{near}(r)
,
\eta_{\alpha,\beta}(x)\leq 1-\frac{15}{32}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{j}^{0})^{2}
.

With the same reasoning, for all
x\in\mathcal{G}_{x_{j}^{0}}(\mathcal{X}_{j}^{near}(r))
we can obtain
\left\lVert D_{2}[\eta_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}](x)-K^{(02)}_{\rm norm}(x_{j}^{0},x)\right\rVert_{x}\leq\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}}{16}
. So from Lemma
I.2
, for all
x\in\mathcal{X}_{j}^{near}(r)
we have
|1-\eta_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}(x)|\leq\frac{B_{02}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}/16}{2}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{j}^{0})^{2}
. We used that
B_{02}\geq\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}
.

We also have, for
i\neq j
, and
x\in\mathcal{G}_{x_{i}^{0}}(\mathcal{X}_{i}^{near}(r))
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{2}[\eta_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}](x)\right\rVert_{x}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\alpha_{i}^{j}K^{(02)}_{\rm norm}(x_{i}^{0},x)+\sum_{l\neq i}\alpha_{l}^{j}K^{(02)}_{\rm norm}(x_{l}^{0},x)+[\beta_{i}^{j}]K^{(12)}_{\rm norm}(x_{i}^{0},x)+\sum_{l\neq i}[\beta_{l}^{j}]K^{(12)}_{\rm norm}(x_{l}^{0},x)\right\rVert_{x}\,,

\displaystyle\leq|\alpha_{i}^{j}|B_{02}+\left\lVert\alpha^{j}\right\rVert_{\infty}h+\max_{i}\left\lVert\beta_{i}^{j}\right\rVert_{x_{i}^{0}}(B_{12}+h)\,,

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2h}{1-2h}B_{02}+\frac{h}{1-2h}+4h(B_{12}+h)\,,

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}}{16}\,.

Lemma
I.2
ensures that for all
x\in\mathcal{X}_{i}^{near}(r)
,
|\eta_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}(x)|\leq\frac{\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}}{16}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{g}}(x,x_{i}^{0})^{2}
.
This concludes the proof.
∎

Norm of the certificates

We use bounds on the norm of the certificates to get the controls in estimation and in prediction (
c_{p}
in Assumption
1
). The construction of certificates presented in Lemma
I.1
allows us to obtain the bounds displayed in the following proposition.

Proposition I.1
.

The certificates constructed in Lemma
I.1
verify, for all
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
, for all
j\penalty\ \in\penalty\ \{1,\ldots,s\}
,

\eta_{\alpha,\beta}(x)=\left\langle\Psi\delta_{x},p_{\alpha,\beta}\right\rangle_{\mathbb{L}}\quad\text{and}\quad\eta_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}(x)=\left\langle\Psi\delta_{x},p_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}\right\rangle_{\mathbb{L}}\quad\text{with}\quad\left\lVert p_{\alpha,\beta}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}\leq\sqrt{2s}\,,\quad\left\lVert p_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}\leq\sqrt{2}\,.

Proof.

From Lemma
5.1
, for all
j\penalty\ \in\penalty\ \{1,\ldots,s\}
,

p_{\alpha,\beta}=\sum_{i=1}^{s}\alpha_{i}\Psi\delta_{x_{i}^{0}}+\sum_{i=1}^{s}\beta_{i}\nabla_{x}\left(\Psi\delta_{x_{i}^{0}}\right)

and

p_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}=\sum_{i=1}^{s}\alpha_{i}^{j}\Psi\delta_{x_{i}^{0}}+\sum_{i=1}^{s}\beta_{i}^{j}\nabla_{x}\left(\Psi\delta_{x_{i}^{0}}\right)\,.

So recalling (
63
) and defining

D_{\mathfrak{g}}\coloneq\begin{pmatrix}\operatorname{Id}_{s}&&&&\\
&&\mathfrak{g}_{x_{1}^{0}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}&&\\
&&&\ddots&\\
&&&&\mathfrak{g}_{x_{s}^{0}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\end{pmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{s(d+1)\times s(d+1)}\,,\quad\tilde{\Upsilon}\coloneq D_{\mathfrak{g}}\Upsilon D_{\mathfrak{g}}\,,

using the results of
(
Poon et al. 2023
, p. 268)
we have
\left\lVert p_{\alpha,\beta}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}=\begin{pmatrix}\alpha\\
\beta\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Upsilon\begin{pmatrix}\alpha\\
\beta\end{pmatrix}=\bm{u}_{s}^{T}\tilde{\Upsilon}^{-1}\bm{u}_{s}
.
We can apply
(
Poon et al. 2023
, Lemma 3)
that gives
\left\lVert\tilde{\Upsilon}^{-1}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 2
. So
\left\lVert p_{\alpha,\beta}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}\leq\left\lVert\tilde{\Upsilon}^{-1}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert\bm{u}_{s}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\leq 2s
.
We repeat the same reasoning to bound
\left\lVert p_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}
: we have
\left\lVert p_{\alpha^{j},\beta^{j}}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}=(\bm{u}_{s}^{j})^{T}\tilde{\Upsilon}^{-1}\bm{u}_{s}^{j}\leq\left\lVert\tilde{\Upsilon}^{-1}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert\bm{u}_{s}^{j}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\leq 2
.
∎

The combination of Theorem
I.1
and Proposition
I.1
concludes the proof of Theorem
5.2
.

Appendix J
Proof of Theorem
5.3

Lemma J.1
.

Let
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
.
We can establish that

\displaystyle\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(00)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=|K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})|\,,

\displaystyle\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(10)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\mathfrak{g}_{x}^{-1/2}\nabla_{1}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{2}\,,

\displaystyle\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(11)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\mathfrak{g}_{x}^{-1/2}\nabla_{1}\nabla_{2}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\right\rVert_{2}\,,

\displaystyle\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(02)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\right\rVert_{2}\,,

\displaystyle\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(12)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2d}\max_{k=1,\ldots d}\left\{\left\lVert\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}t_{k}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{t_{k}}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\right\rVert_{2}\right.\,,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\left.\left\lVert\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}u_{k}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{u_{k}}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\right\rVert_{2}\right\}\,.

Proof.

To get the simplified expressions for the operator norms (Definition
5.1
), we use that
\left\lVert v\right\rVert_{x}^{2}\coloneq v^{T}\mathfrak{g}_{x}v
. The result for
\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(10)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}},\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(11)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}},\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(02)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}
is stated by
(
Poon et al. 2023
, Equation (27))
.

To deal with
\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(12)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}
, we also use that our metric is diagonal. Denoting
\tilde{q}=\sqrt{\mathfrak{g}_{x}}q
,
\tilde{V}_{1}=\sqrt{\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}}V_{1}
,
\tilde{V}_{2}=\sqrt{\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}}V_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(12)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\left\lVert V_{1}\right\rVert_{x^{\prime}},\left\lVert V_{2}\right\rVert_{x^{\prime}}\leq 1\\
\left\lVert q=(q_{1},\ldots q_{2d})\right\rVert_{x}\leq 1\end{subarray}}\sum_{k=1}^{d}q_{k}V_{1}^{T}\partial_{t_{k}}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})V_{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{d}q_{k+d}V_{1}^{T}\partial_{u_{k}}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})V_{2}\,,

\displaystyle=\sup_{\left\lVert\tilde{V}_{1}\right\rVert_{2}\,,\>\left\lVert\tilde{V}_{2}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 1\,,\>\left\lVert\tilde{q}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 1}\bigg(\sum_{k=1}^{d}\tilde{q}_{k}\tilde{V}_{1}^{T}\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}t_{k}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{t_{k}}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\tilde{V}_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{d}\tilde{q}_{k+d}\tilde{V}_{1}^{T}\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}u_{k}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{u_{k}}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\tilde{V}_{2}\bigg)\,,

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2d}\max_{k=1,\ldots d}\left\{\sup_{\left\lVert\tilde{V}_{1}\right\rVert_{2},\left\lVert\tilde{V}_{2}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 1}\left|\tilde{V}_{1}^{T}\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}t_{k}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{t_{k}}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\tilde{V}_{2}\right|\right.\,,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.\sup_{\left\lVert\tilde{V}_{1}\right\rVert_{2},\left\lVert\tilde{V}_{2}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 1}\left|\tilde{V}_{1}^{T}\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}u_{k}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{u_{k}}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\tilde{V}_{2}\right|\right\}\,,

giving the desired result.
∎

Global controls

To check the first condition of Definition
5.2
, we give global controls for the Riemannian derivatives of the kernel. We take advantage of the metric used, which allows us to have uniform bounds on
\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
.

Lemma J.2

(Global controls)
.

For
(i,j)\in\{0,1\}\times\{0,1,2\}
we have

\underset{x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}}{\sup}\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(ij)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}\leq B_{ij}

with

\displaystyle B_{00}

\displaystyle=1\,,

\displaystyle B_{10}=B_{01}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2d}\,,

\displaystyle B_{11}

\displaystyle=2d\,,

\displaystyle B_{02}=B_{20}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{4d^{2}+10d}\,,

\displaystyle B_{12}=B_{21}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2d}B_{02}\,.

Proof.

The bounds are based on the expressions given in Lemma
J.1
. We make use of the relation between the Frobenius norm and the
2
-norm:

\forall\,M=(m_{ij})_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq n\\
1\leq j\leq m\end{subarray}}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times m}\,,\quad\left\lVert M\right\rVert_{2}\leq\sqrt{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq n\\
1\leq j\leq m\end{subarray}}m_{ij}^{2}}\leq\sqrt{nm\max_{i,j}m_{ij}^{2}}\,.

Let
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
.

B_{00}
:
Using Cauchy-Schwarz and since the kernel is normalized, we have

|K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})|=|\left\langle\Psi\delta_{x},\Psi\delta_{x^{\prime}}\right\rangle_{\mathbb{L}}|\leq\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}}\left\lVert\Psi\delta_{x}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}=\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x)=1\,.

B_{10}
:
We use that
\mathfrak{g}_{x}
is diagonal, and name its diagonal elements by
\operatorname{diag}(\mathfrak{g}_{t_{1}t_{1}},\ldots,\mathfrak{g}_{t_{d}t_{d}},\mathfrak{g}_{u_{1}u_{1}},\ldots,\mathfrak{g}_{u_{d}u_{d}})\penalty\ =\penalty\ \mathfrak{g}_{x}
. Remark that
\nabla_{1}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})=\left\langle\nabla\Psi\delta_{x},\Psi\delta_{x^{\prime}}\right\rangle_{\mathbb{L}}
along with, for all
k\in\{1,\ldots d\}
,
\left\lVert\partial_{t_{k}}\Psi\delta_{x}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}=\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}t_{k}}^{1/2}
and
\left\lVert\partial_{u_{k}}\Psi\delta_{x}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}=\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}u_{k}}^{1/2}
. We use Cauchy-Schwarz to write

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathfrak{g}_{x}^{-1/2}\nabla_{1}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{d}\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}t_{k}}^{-1}\left\langle\partial_{t_{k}}\Psi\delta_{x},\Psi\delta_{x^{\prime}}\right\rangle_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{d}\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}u_{k}}^{-1}\left\langle\partial_{u_{k}}\Psi\delta_{x},\Psi\delta_{x^{\prime}}\right\rangle_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{d}\left\lVert\Psi\delta_{x^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{d}\left\lVert\Psi\delta_{x^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2d}\,.

B_{11}
:
We use the same reasoning. With
\mathfrak{g}_{x}^{-1/2}\nabla_{1}\nabla_{2}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\eqcolon(m_{ij})_{1\leq i,j\leq 2d}
, we have
m_{ij}
of the form
\mathfrak{g}_{b_{k}b_{k}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{b_{l}b_{l}}^{-1/2}\left\langle\partial_{b_{k}}\Psi\delta_{x},\partial_{b_{l}}\Psi\delta_{x^{\prime}}\right\rangle_{\mathbb{L}}
where
b_{m}
stands for
u_{m}
or
t_{m}
,
1\leq m\leq d
.
So

m_{ij}^{2}\leq\mathfrak{g}_{b_{k}b_{k}}^{-1}\mathfrak{g}_{b_{l}b_{l}}^{-1}\left\lVert\partial_{b_{k}}\Psi\delta_{x}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}\left\lVert\partial_{b_{l}}\Psi\delta_{x^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}\leq 1\,,

from which we conclude that
\left\lVert M\right\rVert_{2}\leq\sqrt{4d^{2}}=2d
.

B_{02}
:
Here, we denote
(m_{ij})_{1\leq i,j\leq 2d}=\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}
. Recall that

H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})=\nabla_{2}^{2}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})-\sum_{k=1}^{d}\Gamma^{t_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})-\sum_{k=1}^{d}\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\,.

Using that
\Gamma^{b_{k}}{}_{b_{l}b_{m}}\neq 0
implies that
b_{k}=b_{l}=t_{k},b_{m}=u_{k}
or
b_{k}=b_{m}=t_{k},b_{l}=u_{k}
or
b_{k}=u_{k},b_{l}=b_{m}=t_{k}
or
b_{k}=b_{l}=b_{m}=u_{k}
, we can treat the
4d
terms
m_{ii},m_{i2i},m_{2ii},m_{2i2i}
(
i\in\{1,\ldots d\}
) separately from the rest of the matrix.

The other terms are of the form

\mathfrak{g}_{b_{k}^{\prime}b_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{b_{l}^{\prime}b_{l}^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{b_{k}^{\prime}b_{l}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})

where
k\neq l
(
i.e
.
the derivatives are associated with different dimensions).
By abuse of notation, we denote
K_{\rm norm}(x_{m},x_{m}^{\prime})
an evaluation of the kernel in dimension
d=1
at points
x_{m}=(t_{m},u_{m}),x_{m}^{\prime}=(t_{m}^{\prime},u_{m}^{\prime})
, and we consider in a similar way
\Psi\delta_{x_{m}}
. Remark that
\mathfrak{g}_{b_{m}b_{m}}=\left\lVert\partial_{b_{m}}\Psi\delta_{x_{m}}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}
.
As
K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})=\prod_{m=1}^{d}K_{\rm norm}(x_{m},x_{m}^{\prime})
, for
k\neq l
we have

\partial_{b_{k}^{\prime}b_{l}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})=\partial_{b_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})\partial_{b_{l}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{l},x_{l}^{\prime})\prod_{m\notin\{k,l\}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{m},x_{m}^{\prime})\,.

(65)

So using Cauchy-Schwarz, for
m_{ij}
such that
i\neq j
and
i\neq 2j
,
j\neq 2i
,

m_{ij}^{2}\leq\mathfrak{g}_{b_{k}^{\prime}b_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1}\mathfrak{g}_{b_{l}^{\prime}b_{l}^{\prime}}^{-1}\left(\partial_{b_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})\right)^{2}\left(\partial_{b_{l}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{l},x_{l}^{\prime})\right)^{2}\prod_{m\notin\{k,l\}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{m},x_{m}^{\prime})^{2}\leq 1\,.

This quantity bounds
(2d)^{2}-4d
squared coefficients of
M
.

The terms
m_{ii}
,
m_{i2i}
and
m_{2ii}
,
m_{2i2i}
(
i\in\{1,\ldots d\}
) are of the form

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1}\left(\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})-\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\right)

(66)

or

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\left(\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})-\Gamma^{t_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\right)

(67)

or

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1}\left(\partial_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})-\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\right)\,.

(68)

These forms concern respectively
d
,
2d
,
d
coefficients. We can again reduce the problem to dimension 1 using the decomposition of the kernel:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})-\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})=\left(\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})-\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})\right)\prod_{l\neq k}K_{\rm norm}(x_{l},x_{l}^{\prime})\,,\\
&\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})-\Gamma^{t_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})=\left(\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})-\Gamma^{t_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})\right)\prod_{l\neq k}K_{\rm norm}(x_{l},x_{l}^{\prime})\,,\\
&\partial_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})-\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})=\left(\partial_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})-\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})\right)\prod_{l\neq k}K_{\rm norm}(x_{l},x_{l}^{\prime})\,.\end{split}

(69)

So for the first form (
66
),

\displaystyle m_{ii}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{-2}\left\langle\Psi\delta_{x},\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}\Psi\delta_{x^{\prime}}-\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{u_{k}^{\prime}}\Psi\delta_{x^{\prime}}\right\rangle_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}\,,

\displaystyle\leq\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{-2}\left\lVert\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}\Psi\delta_{x_{k}^{\prime}}-\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{u_{k}^{\prime}}\Psi\delta_{x_{k}^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}\,,

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{-2}\left(\partial_{t_{k}t_{k}t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k}^{\prime},x_{k}^{\prime})+\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{2}\partial_{u_{k}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k}^{\prime},x_{k}^{\prime})-2\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{t_{k}t_{k}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k}^{\prime},x_{k}^{\prime})\right)\,,

\displaystyle=1

where
\partial_{t_{k}}K_{\rm norm}
(resp.
\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}
) denotes a derivative w.r.t.
the first (resp. the second) variable of the kernel. We can calculate this quantity, which is constant equal to
1
(see
(
Giard 2025
, Section IV)
).
In a similar way, for the second form (
67
) we have

\displaystyle m_{i2i}^{2},m_{2ii}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1}\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1}\left\lVert\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}\Psi\delta_{x_{k}^{\prime}}-\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{t_{k}^{\prime}}\Psi\delta_{x_{k}^{\prime}}\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}\,,

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1}\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1}\left(\partial_{t_{k}u_{k}t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k}^{\prime},x_{k}^{\prime})+\Gamma^{t_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{2}\partial_{t_{k}t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k}^{\prime},x_{k}^{\prime})-2\Gamma^{t_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{t_{k}u_{k}t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k}^{\prime},x_{k}^{\prime})\right)\,,

\displaystyle=3\,.

For the third one (
68
),

\displaystyle m_{2i2i}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{-2}\left(\partial_{u_{k}u_{k}u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k}^{\prime},x_{k}^{\prime})+\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{2}\partial_{u_{k}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k}^{\prime},x_{k}^{\prime})-2\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{u_{k}u_{k}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k}^{\prime},x_{k}^{\prime})\right)\,,

\displaystyle=7\,.

Hence
\left\lVert M\right\rVert_{2}\leq\sqrt{4d^{2}-4d+d+2\times 3d+7d}=\sqrt{4d^{2}+10d}
.

B_{12}
:
Lemma
J.1
gives

\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(12)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}\leq\sqrt{2d}\max_{t_{k},u_{k}\,,\,k=1,\ldots d}\left\{\left\lVert\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}t_{k}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{t_{k}}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\right\rVert_{2}\,,\,\left\lVert\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}u_{k}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{u_{k}}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\right\rVert_{2}\right\}\,.

For any
k\in\{1,\ldots,d\}
,
b_{k}=t_{k}
or
b_{k}=u_{k}
, the coefficients of the matrix
M_{k}=\mathfrak{g}_{b_{k}b_{k}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{b_{k}}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}
are of the form

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}_{b_{m}b_{m}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{b_{l}^{\prime}b_{l}^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{b_{k}^{\prime}b_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{b_{m}b_{l}^{\prime}b_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\quad\text{where}\;\;k\neq l

or

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}_{b_{m}b_{m}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1}\left(\partial_{b_{m}t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})-\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{b_{m}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\right)

or

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}_{b_{m}b_{m}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\left(\partial_{b_{m}t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})-\Gamma^{t_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{b_{m}t_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\right)

or

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}_{b_{m}b_{m}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1}\left(\partial_{b_{m}u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})-\Gamma^{u_{k}^{\prime}}{}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}\partial_{b_{m}u_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\right)\,.

These forms correspond respectively to
(2d)^{2}-4d
,
d
,
2d
,
d
coefficients.
We can bound them with the same arguments as before. We obtain
\left\lVert M_{k}\right\rVert_{2}\leq B_{02}
, so
\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(12)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}\leq\sqrt{2d}B_{02}
.
∎

Controls when
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})
is small

Lemma J.3

(Curvature constants in dimension
d=1
)
.

Let
r>0
. Let
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)
. If
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\geq r
, then
K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\leq 1-\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}(r)
where
\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}(r)\leq 1-e^{-r^{2}/2}
.
Moreover, if
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\leq r
where
r\leq 0.32
, then

-v^{T}H^{\mathfrak{g}}_{2}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})v\geq\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}(r)\left\lVert v\right\rVert_{x^{\prime}}^{2}\quad\forall\,v\in\mathbb{R}^{2}

where
\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}(r)\leq e^{-r^{2}/2}|G(r)|
(
G(r)
defined by (
76
) below).

Proof.

Obtaining
\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}
:
By definition of the semi-distance (see (
21
)),
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\geq r
implies that
K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\penalty\ \leq\penalty\ e^{-r^{2}/2}
.

Obtaining
\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}
, general overview:

We use that

-v^{T}H^{\mathfrak{g}}_{2}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})v\geq\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}\left\lVert v\right\rVert_{x^{\prime}}^{2}\;\;\forall\,v\in\mathbb{R}^{2}\quad\iff\quad-v^{T}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}H^{\mathfrak{g}}_{2}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}v\geq\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}\left\lVert v\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\;\;\forall\,v\in\mathbb{R}^{2}\,.

Defining
\tilde{H}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})\coloneq K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})^{-1}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}H^{\mathfrak{g}}_{2}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}
, we have (see
(
Giard 2025
, Section V.1)
)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\tilde{H}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})&=\begin{pmatrix}\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}t^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})&\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})\\
\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})&\tilde{H}_{u^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})\end{pmatrix}\quad\text{where}\\
\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}t^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})&=-1\,,\\
\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})&=\frac{(t-t^{\prime})^{3}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3/2}}{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}-\frac{3(t-t^{\prime})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{1/2}(u^{\prime 2}-u^{2})}{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}\,,\\
\tilde{H}_{u^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})&=\frac{(t-t^{\prime})^{4}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{4}}+\frac{3(t-t^{\prime})^{2}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}+\frac{(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})^{2}}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}-\frac{(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}\,.\end{split}

(70)

If the maximal eigenvalue
\lambda
of
\tilde{H}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})
is smaller than some
c<0
, as
K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\geq e^{-r^{2}/2}
,

-v^{T}H^{\mathfrak{g}}_{2}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})v\geq-\lambda K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\left\lVert v\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}\geq-ce^{-r^{2}/2}\left\lVert v\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}}^{2}

and we can take
\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}(r)=-e^{-r^{2}/2}c
.
It remains to bound
\lambda
. We use that

\lambda\leq\max\{\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}t^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime}),\tilde{H}_{u^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})\}+|\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})|\,.

We will provide control of the three terms in the right-hand side of the previous inequality.

Basic inequalities:

Using that
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\leq r
, we have

\frac{|t-t^{\prime}|}{\sqrt{u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}\leq r

(71)

and

\frac{u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}{\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\sqrt{2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}\leq e^{r^{2}}\,.

Using (
35
), we get

\frac{|u^{2}-{u^{\prime}}^{2}|}{u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}\leq\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\,.

(72)

From (
36
) we also have

\frac{2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}{u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}\leq e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\,.

(73)

Control of
\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{02}
:
Using (
71
), (
72
) and (
73
), we get from (
70
) that

\displaystyle|\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle\leq r^{3}\frac{(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3/2}}{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3/2}}+3r\frac{(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{1/2}|u^{2}-u^{\prime 2}|}{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3/2}}\,,

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}r^{3}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})^{3/2}+\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}r\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\sqrt{e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}}\,.

(74)

Control of
\tilde{H}_{u^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{02}
:

As
\frac{\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\sqrt{2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}{u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}\geq e^{-r^{2}}
,

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{u^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle\leq r^{4}\frac{(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}+3r^{2}\frac{(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})|u^{2}-u^{\prime 2}|}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}+\frac{(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})^{2}}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}-\frac{(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}r^{4}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})^{2}+3r^{2}\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})+\frac{1}{2}(e^{2r^{2}}-1)-e^{-2r^{2}}

(75)

where we have used (
71
), (
72
) and (
73
) again.

Conclusion:

The previous bound is greater than
\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}t^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})=-1
(because
-e^{-2r^{2}}\geq-1)
. It comes that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\lambda&\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}r^{3}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})^{3/2}+\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}r\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\sqrt{e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}}\\
&\quad+\frac{1}{2}r^{4}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})^{2}+3r^{2}\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})+\frac{1}{2}(e^{2r^{2}}-1)-e^{-2r^{2}}\eqcolon G(r)\,.\end{split}

(76)

The function
G
is non-decreasing on
\mathbb{R}^{+}
as a sum of non-decreasing functions. It is negative for
r\leq 0.32
(see
(
Giard 2025
, Section V.2)
).
Then
\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}(r)
can be chosen as
-e^{-r^{2}/2}G(r)=e^{-r^{2}/2}|G(r)|
(or smaller) for
r\in[0,0.32]
.
∎

Lemma J.4

(Curvature constants in dimension
d\geq 1
)
.

Let
r\geq 0
. Let
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
. If
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\geq r
, then
K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\leq 1-\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}(r)
where
\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}(r)\leq 1-e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{2}}
.
Moreover, if
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\leq r
where
r=\frac{r_{0}}{\sqrt{d}}
with
0<r_{0}\leq 0.32
, then

-vH^{\mathfrak{g}}_{2}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})v\geq\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}(r)\left\lVert v\right\rVert_{x^{\prime}}^{2}\quad\forall\,v\in\mathbb{R}^{2}

where
\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}(r)\leq e^{-\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{2d}}|G(r_{0})|
(
G(r_{0})
defined by (
76
)).

Proof.

To get
\bar{\varepsilon}_{0}(r)
, remark again that
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\geq r
implies that
K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\leq e^{-r^{2}/2}
(see (
21
)).

Reduction to dimension
1
:
For
\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}(r)
, we use the same reasoning as in Lemma
J.3
.

We denote
\tilde{H}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})\coloneq K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})^{-1}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}H^{\mathfrak{g}}_{2}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}
. Its maximum eigenvalue
\lambda
is smaller than

\max_{1\leq i\leq 2d}\{\tilde{H}_{ii}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})+\sum_{j\neq i}|\tilde{H}_{ij}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})|\}
.
We denote
\tilde{H}_{ij}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})=\tilde{H}_{b_{k}^{\prime}b_{l}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})
to specify that this coefficient corresponds to the derivatives w.r.t.

b_{k}^{\prime},b_{l}^{\prime}
where
1\leq k,l\leq d
(
b
can be
u
or
t
).

We then remark that we can reduce the problem to dimension 1. First, according to (
21
),
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\leq r
implies that
\mathpzc{d}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})\leq r
for all
k=1,\ldots,d
. Using (
65
), we get for
l\neq k

\displaystyle|\tilde{H}_{b_{k}^{\prime}b_{l}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle\leq|\mathfrak{g}_{b_{k}^{\prime}b_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{b_{l}^{\prime}b_{l}^{\prime}}^{-1/2}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})^{-1}K_{\rm norm}(x_{l},x_{l}^{\prime})^{-1}\partial_{b_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})\partial_{b_{l}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{l},x_{l}^{\prime})|\,,

\displaystyle=|\mathfrak{g}_{b_{k}^{\prime}b_{k}^{\prime}}^{-1/2}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})^{-1}\partial_{b_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})||\mathfrak{g}_{b_{l}^{\prime}b_{l}^{\prime}}^{-1/2}K_{\rm norm}(x_{l},x_{l}^{\prime})^{-1}\partial_{b_{l}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{l},x_{l}^{\prime})|\,.

(77)

For
l=k
, using (
69
) we have

\tilde{H}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})\leq\tilde{H}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{02}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})=-1\,,\quad\tilde{H}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})\leq\tilde{H}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{02}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})\,,\quad|\tilde{H}_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})|\leq|\tilde{H}_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{02}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})|\,.

(78)

Bounds for
\tilde{H}_{b_{k}^{\prime}b_{k}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})
:
We use (
78
). From (
74
) and (
75
), we have

\tilde{H}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{k}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})\vee\tilde{H}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})\leq\frac{1}{2}r^{4}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})^{2}+3r^{2}\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})+\frac{1}{2}(e^{2r^{2}}-1)-e^{-2r^{2}}

and

|\tilde{H}_{t_{k}^{\prime}u_{k}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})|\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}r^{3}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})^{3/2}+\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}r\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\sqrt{e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}}\,.

Bounds for
\tilde{H}_{b_{k}^{\prime}b_{l}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})
,
l\neq k
:
See
(
Giard 2025
, Section V.3)
. We calculate

\mathfrak{g}_{x_{k}}^{-1/2}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})^{-1}\nabla_{1}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{(t_{k}-t_{k}^{\prime})\sqrt{2u_{k}^{2}+\tau^{2}}}{u_{k}^{2}+u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}\\
\frac{(t_{k}-t_{k}^{\prime})^{2}(2u_{k}^{2}+\tau^{2})}{\sqrt{2}(u_{k}^{2}+u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}+\frac{u_{k}^{\prime 2}-u_{k}^{2}}{\sqrt{2}(u_{k}^{2}+u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})}\end{pmatrix}\,.

With (
71
), (
72
) and (
73
), it follows using (
77
) that for
l\neq k
,

\displaystyle|\tilde{H}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{l}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{|t_{k}^{\prime}-t_{k}|\sqrt{2u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}{u_{k}^{2}+u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}\frac{|t_{l}-t_{l}^{\prime}|\sqrt{2u_{l}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}{u_{l}^{2}+u_{l}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\leq r^{2}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})

and

\displaystyle|\tilde{H}_{u_{k}^{\prime}t_{l}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{|t_{l}^{\prime}-t_{l}|\sqrt{2u_{l}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}{u_{l}^{2}+u_{l}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}\left(\frac{(t_{k}-t_{k}^{\prime})^{2}(2u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})}{\sqrt{2}(u_{k}^{2}+u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}+\frac{|u_{k}^{2}-u_{k}^{\prime 2}|}{\sqrt{2}(u_{k}^{2}+u_{k}^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\leq r\sqrt{e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}}\times\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(r^{2}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\right)

along with

\displaystyle|\tilde{H}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{l}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left(r^{2}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\right)^{2}\,.

Conclusion:
The bound found for
|\tilde{H}_{u_{k}^{\prime}u_{l}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})|
is greater than the one found for
|\tilde{H}_{t_{k}^{\prime}t_{l}^{\prime}}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})|
. In fact, using that
e^{r^{2}}\geq 1+r^{2}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(r^{2}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq r^{2}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}+\frac{1}{2}(e^{2r^{2}}-1)\,,

\displaystyle\geq r^{2}\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}+\frac{1}{2}(2r^{2}+1)(e^{2r^{2}}-1)\,,

\displaystyle\geq r^{2}\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}+\frac{1}{2}(2r^{2}+1)(e^{r^{2}}(1+r^{2})-1)\,,

\displaystyle\geq r^{2}\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}+r^{2}e^{r^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}(r^{2}+1)e^{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}(2r^{2}+1)\,,

\displaystyle\geq r^{2}\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}+r^{2}e^{r^{2}}\,.

Hence

\displaystyle\begin{split}\lambda&\leq\frac{1}{2}r^{4}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})^{2}+3r^{2}\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})+\frac{1}{2}(e^{2r^{2}}-1)-e^{-2r^{2}}\\
&\quad+(d-1)\frac{1}{2}\left(r^{2}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\right)^{2}\\
&\quad+(d-1)r\sqrt{e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}}\times\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(r^{2}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\right)\\
&\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}r^{3}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})^{3/2}+\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}r\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\sqrt{e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}}\,,\end{split}

\displaystyle\begin{split}&=-e^{-2r^{2}}+2r^{2}\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})+\frac{d}{2}\left(r^{2}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\right)^{2}\\
&\quad+\frac{dr}{\sqrt{2}}\sqrt{e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}}\left(r^{2}(e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1})+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\right)\\
&\quad+\sqrt{2}r\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\sqrt{e^{r^{2}}+\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}}\eqcolon G_{d}(r)\,.\end{split}

Furthermore,
r\in\mathbb{R}^{+}\mapsto\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}
is convex. In fact, for
r>0
, using that
e^{2r^{2}}\geq 1+2r^{2}
, we have

\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial r^{2}}\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}=\frac{2e^{2r^{2}}(2r^{2}e^{2r^{2}}-4r^{2}+e^{2r^{2}}-1)}{(e^{2r^{2}}-1)^{3/2}}\geq\frac{2e^{2r^{2}}(2r^{2}-4r^{2}+1+2r^{2}-1)}{(e^{2r^{2}}-1)^{3/2}}\geq 0\,.

Let
r_{0}>0
. As
r\coloneq\frac{r_{0}}{\sqrt{d}}\leq r_{0}
,
\sqrt{e^{2r^{2}}-1}\leq\frac{\sqrt{e^{2r_{0}^{2}}-1}}{\sqrt{d}}
and we deduce that
G_{d}(r)\leq G_{1}(r_{0})=G(r_{0})
(see (
76
).)
If
r_{0}\leq 0.32
, this quantity is negative (proof of Lemma
J.3
). We can take
\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}\left(\frac{r_{0}}{\sqrt{d}}\right)=-e^{-\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{2d}}G(r_{0})
. The choice of the dependence on
d
for
r=\frac{r_{0}}{\sqrt{d}}
is intended to compensate for the term
d(e^{2r^{2}}-1)
appearing in
G_{d}(r)
.
∎

Controls when
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})
is large

The constraint
\tau\leq u_{\min}
is used in the following lemma.

Lemma J.5

(Bounds under a large separation, in dimension
d=1
)
.

Let
\Delta>0
. Let
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)
. Assume that
\tau\leq u_{\min}
. If
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\geq\Delta
, then

\max_{(i,j)\in\{0,1\}\times\{0,1,2\}}\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(ij)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}\leq\sqrt{2}\,153.05e^{-\frac{\Delta^{2}}{4}}\,.

Proof.

The calculations in this proof are presented in
(
Giard 2025
, Sections VI.1 and VI.2)
. Let
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)
such that
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\geq\Delta
. The following bounds on the operator norms are based on the simplified expressions from Lemma
J.1
. We will use the inequalities

\frac{\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\sqrt{2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}{u^{\prime 2}+u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\vee\frac{|u^{\prime 2}-u^{2}|}{u^{\prime 2}+u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\leq 1\,,\quad\frac{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}{u^{\prime 2}+u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\leq 2\,,\quad\frac{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}{u^{2}}\leq 3\,,

(79)

which holds since
\tau\leq u_{\min}
. As it holds
y^{q}e^{-\frac{y^{2}}{4}}\leq\left(\frac{2q}{e}\right)^{q/2}
for all
y\geq 0
,
q\geq 1
, we also have that for all
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)
,

\displaystyle\forall\,q\geq 1\,,\quad\frac{|t-t^{\prime}|^{q}}{(u^{\prime 2}+u^{2}+\tau^{2})^{\frac{q}{2}}}\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2q}{e}\right)^{q/2}\frac{(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})^{1/8}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{1/8}}{(u^{\prime 2}+u^{2}+\tau^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}}\,,

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2q}{e}\right)^{q/2}\,.

(80)

We will also use that
\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}\leq e^{-\Delta^{2}/4}
for
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\geq\Delta
(see (
21
)).

In what follows, we bound the
2
-norm of a matrix
M=(m_{ij})_{ij}
by its Frobenius norm
\sqrt{\sum_{i,j}m_{ij}^{2}}
.

\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(00)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert
:
We have
|K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})|\leq e^{-\Delta^{2}/2}
, which is an immediate consequence of (
21
).

\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(10)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x}
:
First,

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}H^{10}_{t}(x,x^{\prime})\\
H^{10}_{u}(x,x^{\prime})\end{pmatrix}=H^{10}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle\coloneq\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}^{-1}\mathfrak{g}_{x}^{-1/2}\nabla_{1}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\,,

\displaystyle=\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}\begin{pmatrix}\frac{(t-t^{\prime})\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}}{u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}\\
\frac{(t-t^{\prime})^{2}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})}{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}+\frac{u^{\prime 2}-u^{2}}{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})}\end{pmatrix}\,.

Using (
80
) and (
79
) along with
\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}\leq 1
, it comes

\displaystyle\begin{split}|H_{t}^{10}(x,x^{\prime})|&\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{e}}\\
\text{and}\quad|H_{u}^{10}(x,x^{\prime})|&\leq\left(\frac{4\sqrt{2}}{e}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)\,,\end{split}

(81)

hence
\left\lVert H^{10}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{2}\leq\sqrt{\frac{4}{e}+\left(\frac{4\sqrt{2}}{e}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)^{2}}
and

\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(10)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x}=\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}\left\lVert H^{10}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{2}\leq e^{-\Delta^{2}/4}\sqrt{\frac{4}{e}+\left(\frac{4\sqrt{2}}{e}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)^{2}}\,.

\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(11)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}
:
We have

H^{11}(x,x^{\prime})\coloneq\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}^{-1}\mathfrak{g}_{x}^{-1/2}\nabla_{1}\nabla_{2}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}=\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}\begin{pmatrix}\tilde{H}_{tt^{\prime}}^{11}(x,x^{\prime})&\tilde{H}_{tu^{\prime}}^{11}(x,x^{\prime})\\
\tilde{H}_{tu^{\prime}}^{11}(x,x^{\prime})&\tilde{H}_{uu^{\prime}}^{11}(x,x^{\prime})\end{pmatrix}

where

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{tt^{\prime}}^{11}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\sqrt{2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}{u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}-\frac{(t-t^{\prime})^{2}\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\sqrt{2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{tu^{\prime}}^{11}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{-(t-t^{\prime})^{3}u^{\prime}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})\sqrt{2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}{\sqrt{2}u(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}(t-t^{\prime})u^{\prime}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})\sqrt{2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}}{u(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(t-t^{\prime})u^{\prime}(u^{\prime 2}-u^{2})(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})}{\sqrt{2}u\sqrt{2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{tu^{\prime}}^{11}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\tilde{H}_{tu^{\prime}}^{11}(x^{\prime},x)\,,

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{uu^{\prime}}^{11}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{(t-t^{\prime})^{4}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{4}}+\frac{-2(t-t^{\prime})^{2}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}+\frac{(t-t^{\prime})^{2}(u^{\prime 2}-u^{2})^{2}}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}-\frac{(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})^{2}}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}\,.

The constraint
\tau\leq u_{\min}
is used here, to control
\frac{\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}}{u}
in
\tilde{H}_{tu^{\prime}}^{11},\tilde{H}_{tu^{\prime}}^{11}
.
Using again (
80
), (
79
) and the normalization
\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}\leq 1
, it comes

\displaystyle\begin{split}|H_{tt^{\prime}}^{11}(x,x^{\prime})|&\leq\left(1+\frac{4}{e}\right)\,,\\
|H_{ut^{\prime}}^{11}(x,x^{\prime})|\vee|H_{tu^{\prime}}^{11}(x,x^{\prime})|&\leq\left(\frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\frac{6}{e}\right)^{3/2}+\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{e}}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{e}}\right)\,,\\
|H_{uu^{\prime}}^{11}(x,x^{\prime})|&\leq\left(\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{8}{e}\right)^{2}+\frac{8}{e}+\frac{4}{e}+1+\frac{1}{2}\right)\,.\end{split}

(82)

Hence
\left\lVert H^{(11)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{2}\leq\sqrt{\left(1+\frac{4}{e}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\frac{6}{e}\right)^{3/2}+\frac{3\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{e}}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{32}{e^{2}}+\frac{12}{e}+\frac{3}{2}\right)^{2}}
and

\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(11)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}\leq e^{-\Delta^{2}/4}\sqrt{\left(1+\frac{4}{e}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\frac{6}{e}\right)^{3/2}+\frac{3\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{e}}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{32}{e^{2}}+\frac{12}{e}+\frac{3}{2}\right)^{2}}\,.

\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(02)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x^{\prime}}
:

We use (
70
) to get the expression of

H^{02}(x,x^{\prime})\coloneq\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}\tilde{H}^{02}(x,x^{\prime})=\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}^{-1}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\,.

Using the same techniques as before, we find
\left\lVert H^{02}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{2}\leq\sqrt{1+2\left(3\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{e}}+2\left(\frac{6}{e}\right)^{3/2}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{128}{e^{2}}+\frac{24}{e}+\frac{3}{2}\right)^{2}}
so

\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(02)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x^{\prime}}\leq e^{-\Delta^{2}/4}\sqrt{1+2\left(3\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{e}}+2\left(\frac{6}{e}\right)^{3/2}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{128}{e^{2}}+\frac{24}{e}+\frac{3}{2}\right)^{2}}\,.

\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(12)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}
:
We denote

H^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})=\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}^{-1}\mathfrak{g}_{tt}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{t}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}

and

H^{12,2}(x,x^{\prime})=\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}^{-1}\mathfrak{g}_{uu}^{-1/2}\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\partial_{u}H_{2}^{\mathfrak{g}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})\mathfrak{g}_{x^{\prime}}^{-1/2}\,.

We find

\tilde{H}^{12,1}=\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}\begin{pmatrix}\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}t^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})&\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})\\
\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})&\tilde{H}_{u^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})\end{pmatrix}

with

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}t^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{(t-t^{\prime})\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}}{u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{3(t-t^{\prime})^{2}\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3/2}}{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}-\frac{(t-t^{\prime})^{4}\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3/2}}{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{4}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{3(t-t^{\prime})^{2}\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\sqrt{2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}(u^{\prime 2}-u^{2})}{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}+\frac{3\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}\sqrt{2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2}}(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})}{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}

and

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{u^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{-(t-t^{\prime})^{5}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{5}}+\frac{2(t-t^{\prime})^{3}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{4}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{3(t-t^{\prime})^{3}(u^{\prime 2}-u^{2})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{4}}-\frac{(t-t^{\prime})\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})^{2}}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(t-t^{\prime})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})^{3/2}}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}+\frac{6(t-t^{\prime})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})\sqrt{2u^{2}+\tau^{2}}}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}\,.

Using the same ideas as before to bound the coefficients, we get

\left\lVert H^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{2}\leq\sqrt{\frac{4}{e}+2\left(\frac{18\sqrt{2}}{e}+\sqrt{2}\frac{64}{e^{2}}+\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}\right)^{2}+\left(\sqrt{2}\left(\frac{10}{e}\right)^{5/2}+7\sqrt{2}\left(\frac{6}{e}\right)^{3/2}+\frac{15}{\sqrt{e}}\right)^{2}}\,.

We also have
\tilde{H}^{12,2}=\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})}\begin{pmatrix}\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}t^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})&\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})\\
\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})&\tilde{H}_{u^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})\end{pmatrix}

with

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}t^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{-(t-t^{\prime})^{2}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})}{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}+\frac{u^{2}-u^{\prime 2}}{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})}\,,

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{t^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{(t-t^{\prime})^{5}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3/2}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{5}}+\frac{(t-t^{\prime})^{3}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{1/2}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{4}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{4(t-t^{\prime})^{3}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3/2}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{4}}+\frac{(t-t^{\prime})^{3}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{1/2}}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{3(t-t^{\prime})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{1/2}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}+\frac{21(t-t^{\prime})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{1/2}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})(u^{\prime 2}-u^{2})}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{3(t-t^{\prime})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{1/2}(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}

and

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{u^{\prime}u^{\prime}}^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})=

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{2}(t-t^{\prime})^{6}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})}{4(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{6}}+\frac{5\sqrt{2}(t-t^{\prime})^{4}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})}{4(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{5}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2\sqrt{2}(t-t^{\prime})^{4}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{5}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}(t-t^{\prime})^{4}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})^{2}}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{5}}

\displaystyle-\frac{5\sqrt{2}(t-t^{\prime})^{2}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})^{2}}{4(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{4}}+\frac{5\sqrt{2}(t-t^{\prime})^{2}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{2}}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{4}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{2}(t-t^{\prime})^{2}(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})^{3}}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{4}}-\frac{7\sqrt{2}(t-t^{\prime})^{2}(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})}{(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{4}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{7\sqrt{2}(2u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})(2u^{2}+\tau^{2})(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})}{2(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}-\frac{\sqrt{2}(u^{2}-u^{\prime 2})^{3}}{4(u^{2}+u^{\prime 2}+\tau^{2})^{3}}\,.

We get

\left\lVert H^{12,2}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{2}\leq\sqrt{\begin{aligned} &\left(\frac{4\sqrt{2}}{e}+1\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\frac{10}{e}\right)^{5/2}+6\sqrt{2}\left(\frac{6}{e}\right)^{3/2}+\frac{36}{\sqrt{e}}\right)^{2}\\
&\>\>+\left(\frac{432\sqrt{2}}{e^{3}}+\frac{272\sqrt{2}}{e^{2}}+\frac{60\sqrt{2}}{e}+\frac{15\sqrt{2}}{4}\right)^{2}\end{aligned}}\leq 153.05\,.

As this bound is greater than the one found for
\left\lVert H^{12,1}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{2}
, we deduce that

\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(12)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}\leq\sqrt{2}\,153.05e^{-\Delta^{2}/4}\,.

This bound is the largest we obtained for
\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(ij)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}
, hence it is an upper bound for all the derivatives investigated by this lemma.
∎

Lemma J.6

(Bounds under a large separation, in dimension
d\geq 1
)
.

Let
\Delta>0
. Let
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
. Assume that
\tau\leq u_{\min}
. If
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\geq\Delta
, then

\max_{(i,j)\in\{0,1\}\times\{0,1,2\}}\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(ij)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x,x^{\prime}}\leq\sqrt{2d}(170.5+25.78d)e^{-\Delta^{2}/4}\,.

Proof.

Let
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[u_{\min},+\infty)^{d}
such that
\mathpzc{d}(x,x^{\prime})\geq\Delta
. We use the decomposition of the kernel
K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})=\prod_{k=1}^{d}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})
to reuse the results proven in dimension 1 (see Lemma
J.5
). As in Lemma
J.4
, we use the expressions for the operator norms given in Lemma
J.1
and we bound the
2
-norm of a matrix
M=(m_{ij})_{ij}
by
\sqrt{\sum_{i,j}m_{ij}^{2}}
.
The calculations in this proof are presented in
(
Giard 2025
, Section VI.3)
.

\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(00)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert
:
According to (
21
), we still have
|K_{\rm norm}^{(00)}(x,x^{\prime})|\leq e^{-\Delta^{2}/2}
.

\left\lVert K_{\rm norm}^{(10)}(x,x^{\prime})\right\rVert_{x}
:
We recall that the notation
\partial_{b_{k}}K_{\rm norm}
(resp.
\partial_{b_{k}^{\prime}}K_{\rm norm}
) refers to a derivative w.r.t.
to the first (resp. the second) variable of the kernel, where
b_{k}
is
t_{k}
or
u_{k}
. The decomposition of the kernel gives
\partial_{b_{k}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})=\partial_{b_{k}}K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})\prod_{l\neq k}^{d}K_{\rm norm}(x_{l},x_{l}^{\prime})
. Using (
80
) for
K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})
, we get that

\forall\,q\geq 1\,,\quad\frac{|t_{k}-t_{k}^{\prime}|^{q}}{(u_{k}^{\prime 2}+u_{k}^{2}+\tau^{2})^{\frac{q}{2}}}\sqrt{K_{\rm norm}(x_{k},x_{k}^{\prime})}\leq\left(\frac{2q}{e}\right)^{q/2}\,.

We have

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{g}_{b_{k}b_{k}}^{-1/2}\partial_{b_{k}}K_{\rm norm}(x,x^{\prime})|
